1. Solar power: This type of energy is generated from the sun's rays, which are processed to create electricity. Solar power is a renewable and sustainable energy source that can be used to generate and use energy efficiently.\n2. wind power: wind power is produced by capturing the wind's motion and using it to generate electricity. It is typically achieved by having wind turbines that are strategically placed in areas where the wind is likely to be strong. The turbines capture the wind's force, causing them to turn and generate electrical power. This type of energy is also renewable and sustainable, making it an ideal option for many families looking to reduce their dependency on non-renewable sources of energy. \n3. hydro power: hydro power is produced by utilizing the potential energy stored in water sources such as rivers, streams, and rain. It is usually achieved by having rain or water droplets that fall into a designated area where they can be collected by special equipment. These droplets have been processed in order to separate and extract the hydrogen from the water, allowing for the generation of electrical power from this renewable source.
